Snapshot: 20260413_0502
GaussiAnimate: Reconstruct and Rig Animatable Categories with Level of Dynamics
Authors: Jiaxin Wang, Dongxin Lyu, Zeyu Cai, Zhiyang Dou, Cheng Lin, Anpei Chen, Yuliang Xiu
First: 2026-04-09T17:59:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:59:59+00:00
Comments: Page: https://cookmaker.cn/gaussianimate
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Free-form bones, that conform closely to the surface, can effectively capture non-rigid deformations, but lack a kinematic structure necessary for intuitive control. Thus, we propose a Scaffold-Skin Rigging System, termed "Skelebones", with three key steps: (1) Bones: compress temporally-consistent deformable Gaussians into free-form bones, approximating non-rigid surface deformations; (2) Skeleton: extract a Mean Curvature Skeleton from canonical Gaussians and refine it temporally, ensuring a category-agnostic, motion-adaptive, and topology-correct kinematic structure; (3) Binding: bind the skeleton and bones via non-parametric partwise motion matching (PartMM), synthesizing novel bone motions by matching, retrieving, and blending existing ones. Collectively, these three steps enable us to compress the Level of Dynamics of 4D shapes into compact skelebones that are both controllable and expressive. We validate our approach on both synthetic and real-world datasets, achieving significant improvements in reanimation performance across unseen poses-with 17.3% PSNR gains over Linear Blend Skinning (LBS) and 21.7% over Bag-of-Bones (BoB)-while maintaining excellent reconstruction fidelity, particularly for characters exhibiting complex non-rigid surface dynamics. Our Partwise Motion Matching algorithm demonstrates strong generalization to both Gaussian and mesh representations, especially under low-data regime (~1000 frames), achieving 48.4% RMSE improvement over robust LBS and outperforming GRU- and MLP-based learning methods by >20%. Code will be made publicly available for research purposes at cookmaker.cn/gaussianimate.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GaussiAnimate：基于动态层级的可动画类别重建与骨骼绑定
自由形态骨骼能紧密贴合表面，有效捕捉非刚性形变，但缺乏直观控制所需的运动结构。为此，我们提出一种名为“Skelebones”的骨架-皮肤绑定系统，包含三个关键步骤：(1) 骨骼：将时间一致的可变形高斯压缩为自由形态骨骼，近似非刚性表面形变；(2) 骨架：从规范高斯中提取平均曲率骨架并进行时间优化，确保其类别无关、运动自适应且拓扑正确；(3) 绑定：通过非参数化分部运动匹配（PartMM）将骨架与骨骼绑定，通过匹配、检索和融合现有运动合成新骨骼运动。三步协同可将4D形状的动态层级压缩为紧凑且可控、表达力强的Skelebones。我们在合成与真实数据集上验证，相比线性混合皮肤（LBS）提升17.3% PSNR，相比Bag-of-Bones（BoB）提升21.7%，同时保持高重建保真度，尤其适用于复杂非刚性表面动态角色。Partwise Motion Matching算法在低数据量（约1000帧）下对高斯与网格表示均具强泛化能力，相比鲁棒LBS提升48.4% RMSE，且优于GRU与MLP学习方法20%以上。代码将在cookmaker.cn/gaussianimate公开以供研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for intuitive control over non-rigid deformations while preserving expressive motion, the authors propose Skelebones, a Scaffold-Skin Rigging System that reconstructs animatable 4D shapes via three steps: compressing temporally-consistent deformable Gaussians into free-form bones, extracting and refining a Mean Curvature Skeleton for motion-adaptive kinematics, and binding them via Partwise Motion Matching to synthesize novel motions. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show Skelebones achieves 17.3% higher PSNR than Linear Blend Skinning and 21.7% over Bag-of-Bones in reanimation, with superior reconstruction fidelity for complex dynamics. The Partwise Motion Matching algorithm also generalizes well to low-data regimes, improving RMSE by 48.4% over LBS and outperforming GRU/MLP methods by over 20%.
为实现对非刚性形变的直观控制并保持运动表达力，作者提出Skelebones骨架皮肤绑定系统，包含三步：将时序一致的可变形高斯压缩为自由形态骨骼，从规范高斯中提取并优化平均曲率骨架以构建运动自适应且拓扑正确的运动结构，再通过部分运动匹配算法绑定骨骼与皮肤并合成新运动。实验表明，该方法在合成与真实数据集上显著优于线性混合皮肤与骨骼袋方法，PSNR提升17.3%和21.7%，对复杂非刚性动态重建精度更优。其部分运动匹配算法在低数据量下表现突出，RMSE较LBS提升48.4%，且超越GRU和MLP方法20%以上。
ETCH-X: Robustify Expressive Body Fitting to Clothed Humans with Composable Datasets
Authors: Xiaoben Li, Jingyi Wu, Zeyu Cai, Yu Siyuan, Boqian Li, Yuliang Xiu
First: 2026-04-09T17:59:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:59:59+00:00
Comments: Page: https://xiaobenli00.github.io/ETCH-X/, Code: https://github.com/XiaobenLi00/ETCH-X
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Human body fitting, which aligns parametric body models such as SMPL to raw 3D point clouds of clothed humans, serves as a crucial first step for downstream tasks like animation and texturing. An effective fitting method should be both locally expressive-capturing fine details such as hands and facial features-and globally robust to handle real-world challenges, including clothing dynamics, pose variations, and noisy or partial inputs. Existing approaches typically excel in only one aspect, lacking an all-in-one solution.We upgrade ETCH to ETCH-X, which leverages a tightness-aware fitting paradigm to filter out clothing dynamics ("undress"), extends expressiveness with SMPL-X, and replaces explicit sparse markers (which are highly sensitive to partial data) with implicit dense correspondences ("dense fit") for more robust and fine-grained body fitting. Our disentangled "undress" and "dense fit" modular stages enable separate and scalable training on composable data sources, including diverse simulated garments (CLOTH3D), large-scale full-body motions (AMASS), and fine-grained hand gestures (InterHand2.6M), improving outfit generalization and pose robustness of both bodies and hands. Our approach achieves robust and expressive fitting across diverse clothing, poses, and levels of input completeness, delivering a substantial performance improvement over ETCH on both: 1) seen data, such as 4D-Dress (MPJPE-All, 33.0% ) and CAPE (V2V-Hands, 35.8% ), and 2) unseen data, such as BEDLAM2.0 (MPJPE-All, 80.8% ; V2V-All, 80.5% ). Code and models will be released at https://xiaobenli00.github.io/ETCH-X/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ETCH-X：通过可组合数据集增强着装人体的表达性拟合鲁棒性
人体拟合，即将参数化人体模型（如SMPL）对齐到着装人体的原始3D点云，是动画和纹理等下游任务的关键第一步。有效的拟合方法应兼具局部表达能力（捕捉手部和面部等细节）与全局鲁棒性（应对现实挑战，如衣物动态、姿态变化及噪声或不完整输入）。现有方法通常仅擅长一方面，缺乏统一解决方案。我们升级了ETCH为ETCH-X，采用紧致感知拟合范式过滤衣物动态（“脱衣”），引入SMPL-X增强表达性，并用隐式密集对应（“密集拟合”）替代易受部分数据影响的显式稀疏标记，实现更鲁棒和精细的人体拟合。我们的解耦“脱衣”与“密集拟合”模块化阶段，支持在可组合数据源（如多样模拟服装CLOTH3D、大规模全身运动AMASS、精细手势InterHand2.6M）上分别且可扩展训练，提升服装泛化能力与姿态鲁棒性（身体与手部）。该方法在多样服装、姿态及输入完整性下实现鲁棒且表达性强的拟合，相较ETCH在已见数据（如4D-Dress：MPJPE-All 33.0%；CAPE：V2V-Hands 35.8%）和未见数据（如BEDLAM2.0：MPJPE-All 80.8%；V2V-All 80.5%）均显著提升性能。代码与模型将在https://xiaobenli00.github.io/ETCH-X/发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for body fitting methods that balance local expressiveness and global robustness in clothed humans, the authors enhance ETCH into ETCH-X by introducing a tightness-aware fitting paradigm to remove clothing effects, adopting SMPL-X for improved expressiveness, and replacing sparse markers with implicit dense correspondences for better handling of partial or noisy data. The modular design enables scalable training on composable datasets including CLOTH3D, AMASS, and InterHand2.6M, leading to improved generalization across outfits and poses. Experimental results show significant performance gains over ETCH: on seen data (4D-Dress: MPJPE-All 33.0%, CAPE: V2V-Hands 35.8%) and unseen data (BEDLAM2.0: MPJPE-All 80.8%, V2V-All 80.5%).
为解决现有方法难以同时兼顾局部细节表达与全局鲁棒性的问题，作者将ETCH升级为ETCH-X，通过紧致感知拟合范式去除衣物影响、采用SMPL-X提升表达能力，并用隐式稠密对应替代易受数据缺失影响的稀疏标记，增强对不完整或噪声输入的鲁棒性。模块化设计支持在CLOTH3D、AMASS和InterHand2.6M等可组合数据集上独立扩展训练，显著提升服装泛化与姿态鲁棒性。实验表明，ETCH-X在已见数据（4D-Dress: MPJPE-All 33.0%，CAPE: V2V-Hands 35.8%）和未见数据（BEDLAM2.0: MPJPE-All 80.8%，V2V-All 80.5%）上均显著优于ETCH。
When Numbers Speak: Aligning Textual Numerals and Visual Instances in Text-to-Video Diffusion Models
Authors: Zhengyang Sun, Yu Chen, Xin Zhou, Xiaofan Li, Xiwu Chen, Dingkang Liang, Xiang Bai
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-09T17:59:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:59:57+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR 2026. Project page: https://h-embodvis.github.io/NUMINA
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Text-to-video diffusion models have enabled open-ended video synthesis, but often struggle with generating the correct number of objects specified in a prompt. We introduce NUMINA , a training-free identify-then-guide framework for improved numerical alignment. NUMINA identifies prompt-layout inconsistencies by selecting discriminative self- and cross-attention heads to derive a countable latent layout. It then refines this layout conservatively and modulates cross-attention to guide regeneration. On the introduced CountBench, NUMINA improves counting accuracy by up to 7.4% on Wan2.1-1.3B, and by 4.9% and 5.5% on 5B and 14B models, respectively. Furthermore, CLIP alignment is improved while maintaining temporal consistency. These results demonstrate that structural guidance complements seed search and prompt enhancement, offering a practical path toward count-accurate text-to-video diffusion. The code is available at https://github.com/H-EmbodVis/NUMINA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当数字发声：在文本到视频扩散模型中对齐文本数字与视觉实例
文本到视频扩散模型虽实现了开放式的视频合成，但常难以准确生成提示中指定数量的对象。我们提出NUMINA，一种无需训练的识别-引导框架，以提升数值对齐效果。NUMINA通过选择判别性的自注意力与跨注意力头，推导可计数的潜在布局，识别提示与布局间的不一致；随后保守地优化该布局，并调节跨注意力以引导再生。在新提出的CountBench上，NUMINA在Wan2.1-1.3B模型上将计数准确率提升7.4%，在5B和14B模型上分别提升4.9%和5.5%。同时，CLIP对齐性能得到改善，且保持时间一致性。结果表明，结构化引导可与种子搜索和提示增强互补，为实现计数准确的文本到视频扩散提供实用路径。代码见https://github.com/H-EmbodVis/NUMINA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the common failure of text-to-video models to accurately generate the specified number of objects, the authors propose NUMINA, a training-free framework that identifies and corrects numerical inconsistencies in prompts by analyzing self- and cross-attention heads to derive a countable latent layout. It then conservatively refines this layout and modulates cross-attention to guide video generation. Experiments on CountBench show NUMINA improves counting accuracy by up to 7.4% on Wan2.1-1.3B, and 4.9% and 5.5% on 5B and 14B models respectively, while enhancing CLIP alignment without compromising temporal consistency.
为了解决文本到视频扩散模型在生成视频时难以准确匹配提示中指定物体数量的问题，作者提出了NUMINA，一种无需训练的框架，通过分析自注意力与交叉注意力模式识别并构建可计数的潜在布局，再保守优化该布局并调节交叉注意力以引导生成。在CountBench数据集上，NUMINA在Wan2.1-1.3B模型上将计数准确率提升最高7.4%，在5B和14B模型上分别提升4.9%和5.5%，同时提升CLIP对齐度且保持时间一致性。
Act Wisely: Cultivating Meta-Cognitive Tool Use in Agentic Multimodal Models
Authors: Shilin Yan, Jintao Tong, Hongwei Xue, Xiaojun Tang, Yangyang Wang, Kunyu Shi, Guannan Zhang, Ruixuan Li, Yixiong Zou
First: 2026-04-09T17:59:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:59:57+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://Accio-Lab.github.io/Metis
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The advent of agentic multimodal models has empowered systems to actively interact with external environments. However, current agents suffer from a profound meta-cognitive deficit: they struggle to arbitrate between leveraging internal knowledge and querying external utilities. Consequently, they frequently fall prey to blind tool invocation, resorting to reflexive tool execution even when queries are resolvable from the raw visual context. This pathological behavior precipitates severe latency bottlenecks and injects extraneous noise that derails sound reasoning. Existing reinforcement learning protocols attempt to mitigate this via a scalarized reward that penalizes tool usage. Yet, this coupled formulation creates an irreconcilable optimization dilemma: an aggressive penalty suppresses essential tool use, whereas a mild penalty is entirely subsumed by the variance of the accuracy reward during advantage normalization, rendering it impotent against tool overuse. To transcend this bottleneck, we propose HDPO, a framework that reframes tool efficiency from a competing scalar objective to a strictly conditional one. By eschewing reward scalarization, HDPO maintains two orthogonal optimization channels: an accuracy channel that maximizes task correctness, and an efficiency channel that enforces execution economy exclusively within accurate trajectories via conditional advantage estimation. This decoupled architecture naturally induces a cognitive curriculum-compelling the agent to first master task resolution before refining its self-reliance. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our resulting model, Metis, reduces tool invocations by orders of magnitude while simultaneously elevating reasoning accuracy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：明智行动：在代理多模态模型中培养元认知工具使用
代理多模态模型的出现使系统能够主动与外部环境交互。然而，当前代理存在严重的元认知缺陷：难以在利用内部知识与查询外部工具之间进行权衡。因此，它们常陷入盲目调用工具的病理行为，即使原始视觉上下文足以解答问题，仍会进行反射式工具执行。这种行为导致严重延迟瓶颈，并引入干扰性噪声，破坏合理推理。现有强化学习协议通过标量奖励惩罚工具使用来缓解此问题，但这种耦合形式造成不可调和的优化困境：严厉惩罚抑制必要工具使用，而温和惩罚在优势归一化过程中被准确率奖励的方差所淹没，对工具过度使用无能为力。为突破此瓶颈，我们提出HDPO框架，将工具效率从竞争性标量目标重构为严格条件性目标。通过摒弃奖励标量化，HDPO维持两个正交优化通道：一个准确率通道最大化任务正确性，一个效率通道仅在准确轨迹内通过条件优势估计强制执行执行经济性。这种解耦架构自然诱导认知课程，迫使代理先掌握任务解决能力，再提升自给自足能力。大量评估表明，我们的模型Metis将工具调用次数降低数个数量级，同时提升推理准确性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of current agentic multimodal models that often invoke tools unnecessarily despite accessible visual context, this work introduces HDPO, a decoupled reinforcement learning framework that separates accuracy and efficiency optimization. Instead of penalizing tool use via scalarized rewards, HDPO enforces execution economy conditionally—only within trajectories that have already achieved correct task outcomes. Experimental results show that the resulting model, Metis, drastically reduces tool invocations while simultaneously improving reasoning accuracy, demonstrating that conditional optimization effectively curbs blind tool use without sacrificing performance.
为解决多模态代理模型盲目调用工具的问题，本文提出HDPO框架，将工具效率优化与任务准确性分离。该方法不采用标量奖励惩罚工具使用，而是在已成功完成任务的轨迹中条件性地强制执行经济性，从而避免传统方法中惩罚过强抑制必要工具调用、惩罚过弱被奖励方差淹没的优化困境。实验表明，所提出的模型Metis显著减少工具调用次数，同时提升推理准确性。
SIM1: Physics-Aligned Simulator as Zero-Shot Data Scaler in Deformable Worlds
Authors: Yunsong Zhou, Hangxu Liu, Xuekun Jiang, Xing Shen, Yuanzhen Zhou, Hui Wang, Baole Fang, Yang Tian, Mulin Yu, Qiaojun Yu, Li Ma, Hengjie Li, Hanqing Wang, Jia Zeng, Jiangmiao Pang
First: 2026-04-09T17:59:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:59:52+00:00
Comments: Website: https://internrobotics.github.io/sim1.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Robotic manipulation with deformable objects represents a data-intensive regime in embodied learning, where shape, contact, and topology co-evolve in ways that far exceed the variability of rigids. Although simulation promises relief from the cost of real-world data acquisition, prevailing sim-to-real pipelines remain rooted in rigid-body abstractions, producing mismatched geometry, fragile soft dynamics, and motion primitives poorly suited for cloth interaction. We posit that simulation fails not for being synthetic, but for being ungrounded. To address this, we introduce SIM1, a physics-aligned real-to-sim-to-real data engine that grounds simulation in the physical world. Given limited demonstrations, the system digitizes scenes into metric-consistent twins, calibrates deformable dynamics through elastic modeling, and expands behaviors via diffusion-based trajectory generation with quality filtering. This pipeline transforms sparse observations into scaled synthetic supervision with near-demonstration fidelity. Experiments show that policies trained on purely synthetic data achieve parity with real-data baselines at a 1:15 equivalence ratio, while delivering 90% zero-shot success and 50% generalization gains in real-world deployment. These results validate physics-aligned simulation as scalable supervision for deformable manipulation and a practical pathway for data-efficient policy learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SIM1：物理对齐的模拟器作为可变形世界中的零样本数据扩展器
机器人对可变形物体的操作是具身学习中数据密集型的领域，其中形状、接触和拓扑结构以远超刚体的方式共同演化。尽管仿真有望缓解现实世界数据采集的成本，但主流的仿真到现实管道仍基于刚体抽象，导致几何不匹配、脆弱的软体动力学和不适用于布料交互的运动原语。我们认为，仿真失败并非因其为合成数据，而是因其脱离物理现实。为解决此问题，我们引入SIM1，一种物理对齐的实-仿-实数据引擎，将仿真扎根于物理世界。在有限演示下，系统将场景数字化为度量一致的孪生体，通过弹性建模校准可变形动力学，并借助扩散轨迹生成与质量过滤扩展行为。该流程将稀疏观测转化为近似演示保真的扩展合成监督。实验表明，仅在合成数据上训练的策略在1:15等效比率下与真实数据基线表现相当，同时在真实部署中实现90%零样本成功率和50%泛化增益。这些结果验证了物理对齐仿真作为可变形操作可扩展监督的有效性，以及其作为高效策略学习实用路径的价值。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of rigid-body simulations in handling deformable objects, the paper introduces SIM1, a physics-aligned simulator that grounds synthetic data in real-world physics to enable zero-shot scaling. It digitizes real scenes into metric-consistent virtual twins, calibrates soft dynamics via elastic modeling, and generates diverse trajectories using diffusion-based methods with quality filtering. Experiments demonstrate that policies trained solely on synthetic data match real-data baselines at a 1:15 data ratio, achieving 90% zero-shot success and 50% generalization improvement in real-world deployment.
为解决传统刚体仿真在处理可变形物体时的局限性，作者提出SIM1，一种基于物理对齐的仿真器，通过将虚拟场景与真实物理世界对齐，实现零样本数据扩展。该方法通过弹性建模校准软体动力学，结合扩散模型生成轨迹并进行质量过滤。实验表明，仅使用合成数据训练的策略在1:15数据比例下即可达到真实数据基线性能，实测零样本成功率90%，泛化能力提升50%。
E-3DPSM: A State Machine for Event-Based Egocentric 3D Human Pose Estimation
Authors: Mayur Deshmukh, Hiroyasu Akada, Helge Rhodin, Christian Theobalt, Vladislav Golyanik
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-09T17:59:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:59:52+00:00
Comments: 20 pages; 14 figures and 14 tables; CVPR 2026; project page: https://4dqv.mpi-inf.mpg.de/E-3DPSM/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Event cameras offer multiple advantages in monocular egocentric 3D human pose estimation from head-mounted devices, such as millisecond temporal resolution, high dynamic range, and negligible motion blur. Existing methods effectively leverage these properties, but suffer from low 3D estimation accuracy, insufficient in many applications (e.g., immersive VR/AR). This is due to the design not being fully tailored towards event streams (e.g., their asynchronous and continuous nature), leading to high sensitivity to self-occlusions and temporal jitter in the estimates. This paper rethinks the setting and introduces E-3DPSM, an event-driven continuous pose state machine for event-based egocentric 3D human pose estimation. E-3DPSM aligns continuous human motion with fine-grained event dynamics; it evolves latent states and predicts continuous changes in 3D joint positions associated with observed events, which are fused with direct 3D human pose predictions, leading to stable and drift-free final 3D pose reconstructions. E-3DPSM runs in real-time at 80 Hz on a single workstation and sets a new state of the art in experiments on two benchmarks, improving accuracy by up to 19% (MPJPE) and temporal stability by up to 2.7x. See our project page for the source code and trained models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：E-3DPSM：面向事件驱动的自摄像机3D人体姿态估计的状态机
事件相机在头戴式单目自摄像机3D人体姿态估计中具有毫秒级时间分辨率、高动态范围和几乎无运动模糊等优势。现有方法虽有效利用了这些特性，但在许多应用（如沉浸式VR/AR）中仍存在3D估计精度低的问题。这源于现有方法未充分针对事件流（如其异步性和连续性）进行设计，导致对自遮挡和时间抖动高度敏感。本文重新思考该问题，提出E-3DPSM——一种面向事件驱动的连续姿态状态机，用于事件驱动的自摄像机3D人体姿态估计。E-3DPSM将连续人体运动与细粒度事件动态对齐；它演化潜在状态并预测与观测事件相关的3D关节位置连续变化，这些变化与直接3D人体姿态预测融合，从而实现稳定且无漂移的最终3D姿态重建。E-3DPSM可在单个工作站上实时运行（80Hz），并在两个基准测试中创下新纪录，3D姿态估计精度最高提升19%（MPJPE），时间稳定性提升最高达2.7倍。项目页面提供源代码和训练模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more accurate and stable 3D human pose estimation from event cameras in egocentric settings, this paper introduces E-3DPSM, a state machine designed to exploit the asynchronous and continuous nature of event streams. It aligns human motion with fine-grained event dynamics by evolving latent states and predicting continuous 3D joint position changes, which are fused with direct pose predictions to reduce drift and self-occlusion sensitivity. Evaluated on two benchmarks, E-3DPSM achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving 3D pose accuracy by up to 19% (MPJPE) and temporal stability by up to 2.7x, while running in real-time at 80 Hz on a single workstation.
为提升头戴式设备中基于事件相机的自摄像3D人体姿态估计的精度与稳定性，本文提出E-3DPSM，一种专为事件流设计的状态机。该方法通过将潜状态演化与细粒度事件动态对齐，预测由事件驱动的3D关节位置变化，并与直接姿态预测融合，从而降低漂移和自遮挡敏感性。在两个基准测试中，E-3DPSM达到当前最优性能，MPJPE精度提升最高19%，时间稳定性提升最高2.7倍，且可在单台工作站上实时运行至80Hz。
Scal3R: Scalable Test-Time Training for Large-Scale 3D Reconstruction
Authors: Tao Xie, Peishan Yang, Yudong Jin, Yingfeng Cai, Wei Yin, Weiqiang Ren, Qian Zhang, Wei Hua, Sida Peng, Xiaoyang Guo, Xiaowei Zhou
First: 2026-04-09T17:59:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:59:50+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://zju3dv.github.io/scal3r
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
This paper addresses the task of large-scale 3D scene reconstruction from long video sequences. Recent feed-forward reconstruction models have shown promising results by directly regressing 3D geometry from RGB images without explicit 3D priors or geometric constraints. However, these methods often struggle to maintain reconstruction accuracy and consistency over long sequences due to limited memory capacity and the inability to effectively capture global contextual cues. In contrast, humans can naturally exploit the global understanding of the scene to inform local perception. Motivated by this, we propose a novel neural global context representation that efficiently compresses and retains long-range scene information, enabling the model to leverage extensive contextual cues for enhanced reconstruction accuracy and consistency. The context representation is realized through a set of lightweight neural sub-networks that are rapidly adapted during test time via self-supervised objectives, which substantially increases memory capacity without incurring significant computational overhead. The experiments on multiple large-scale benchmarks, including the KITTI Odometry~\cite{Geiger2012CVPR} and Oxford Spires~\cite{tao2025spires} datasets, demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in handling ultra-large scenes, achieving leading pose accuracy and state-of-the-art 3D reconstruction accuracy while maintaining efficiency. Code is available at https://zju3dv.github.io/scal3r.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Scal3R：面向大规模3D重建的可扩展测试时训练
本文研究从长视频序列重建大规模3D场景的任务。近期的前馈重建模型通过直接从RGB图像回归3D几何结构，在无需显式3D先验或几何约束的情况下取得了良好效果。然而，这些方法在长序列中常因内存容量有限且难以有效捕捉全局上下文线索，导致重建精度和一致性下降。相比之下，人类能自然利用对场景的整体理解来指导局部感知。受此启发，我们提出一种新颖的神经全局上下文表示方法，高效压缩并保留长距离场景信息，使模型能利用丰富上下文线索提升重建精度与一致性。该上下文表示通过一组轻量级神经子网络实现，测试时通过自监督目标快速自适应调整，显著提升内存容量而几乎不增加计算开销。在KITTI Odometry和Oxford Spires等多个大规模基准数据集上的实验表明，我们的方法在处理超大规模场景时表现优异，实现了领先的位姿精度和最先进的3D重建精度，同时保持高效。代码见https://zju3dv.github.io/scal3r。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of feed-forward 3D reconstruction models in maintaining accuracy over long video sequences due to memory constraints and lack of global context, this paper proposes Scal3R, a test-time training framework that dynamically adapts lightweight neural sub-networks to compress and retain long-range scene information. These sub-networks are trained via self-supervised objectives during inference, enabling the model to leverage global contextual cues without increasing computational cost. Experiments on KITTI Odometry and Oxford Spires show Scal3R achieves state-of-the-art 3D reconstruction accuracy and pose estimation while efficiently handling ultra-large scenes.
为了解决现有前馈3D重建模型在长视频序列中因内存限制和缺乏全局上下文导致的精度下降问题，本文提出Scal3R方法，通过在测试时自监督学习快速适应轻量级神经全局上下文表示，压缩长距离场景信息以提升重建一致性和精度，且计算开销小。在KITTI Odometry和Oxford Spires等大规模数据集上的实验表明，Scal3R在处理超大规模场景时实现了最先进的3D重建精度和领先的位姿精度。
Seeing but Not Thinking: Routing Distraction in Multimodal Mixture-of-Experts
Authors: Haolei Xu, Haiwen Hong, Hongxing Li, Rui Zhou, Yang Zhang, Longtao Huang, Hui Xue, Yongliang Shen, Weiming Lu, Yueting Zhuang
First: 2026-04-09T17:59:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:59:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have achieved remarkable performance on vision-language tasks. However, we identify a puzzling phenomenon termed Seeing but Not Thinking: models accurately perceive image content yet fail in subsequent reasoning, while correctly solving identical problems presented as pure text. Through systematic analysis, we first verify that cross-modal semantic sharing exists in MoE architectures, ruling out semantic alignment failure as the sole explanation. We then reveal that visual experts and domain experts exhibit layer-wise separation, with image inputs inducing significant routing divergence from text inputs in middle layers where domain experts concentrate. Based on these findings, we propose the Routing Distraction hypothesis: when processing visual inputs, the routing mechanism fails to adequately activate task-relevant reasoning experts. To validate this hypothesis, we design a routing-guided intervention method that enhances domain expert activation. Experiments on three multimodal MoE models across six benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements, with gains of up to 3.17% on complex visual reasoning tasks. Our analysis further reveals that domain expert identification locates cognitive functions rather than sample-specific solutions, enabling effective transfer across tasks with different information structures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：看见却不思考：多模态混合专家中的路由分心现象
多模态混合专家（MoE）模型在视觉-语言任务中取得了显著性能。然而，我们发现一种令人困惑的现象，称为“看见却不思考”：模型能准确感知图像内容，但在后续推理中表现不佳，而当相同问题以纯文本形式呈现时，模型却能正确解答。通过系统分析，我们首先验证了MoE架构中存在跨模态语义共享，排除了语义对齐失败是唯一原因的可能性。接着我们揭示了视觉专家与领域专家在层间存在分离，图像输入在领域专家集中分布的中间层中，会引发与文本输入显著不同的路由偏差。基于这些发现，我们提出“路由分心”假说：处理视觉输入时，路由机制未能充分激活与任务相关的推理专家。为验证该假说，我们设计了一种路由引导干预方法，以增强领域专家激活。在三个多模态MoE模型和六个基准测试中的实验表明，该方法在复杂视觉推理任务上带来一致提升，最高提升达3.17%。我们的分析进一步表明，领域专家识别定位的是认知功能而非样本特定解法，从而实现了不同信息结构任务间的有效迁移。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that multimodal MoE models perform well on vision-language tasks but fail at visual reasoning despite accurately perceiving images, the study investigates a phenomenon termed 'Seeing but Not Thinking.' Through systematic analysis, it confirms cross-modal semantic sharing and identifies layer-wise routing divergence between visual and text inputs, particularly in middle layers where domain experts reside. The authors propose the 'Routing Distraction' hypothesis, suggesting visual inputs fail to activate relevant reasoning experts. To test this, they introduce a routing-guided intervention that boosts domain expert activation, achieving up to 3.17% performance gains on complex visual reasoning benchmarks across three models. Their findings further show that domain expert identification reflects general cognitive functions rather than task-specific solutions, enabling transfer across diverse information structures.
针对多模态MoE模型在视觉语言任务中表现优异但视觉推理能力不足的现象，作者提出‘看见却未思考’的假设。通过系统分析发现，跨模态语义共享存在，排除语义对齐失败的解释，并揭示视觉输入在中间层会引发与文本输入不同的路由偏差，导致领域专家未被充分激活。基于‘路由干扰’假设，作者设计路由引导干预方法增强领域专家激活，实验证明在三个模型和六个基准上均取得提升，最高达3.17%，且领域专家识别反映的是认知功能而非样本特异性解法，支持其在不同信息结构任务间有效迁移。
AVGen-Bench: A Task-Driven Benchmark for Multi-Granular Evaluation of Text-to-Audio-Video Generation
Authors: Ziwei Zhou, Zeyuan Lai, Rui Wang, Yifan Yang, Zhen Xing, Yuqing Yang, Qi Dai, Lili Qiu, Chong Luo
First: 2026-04-09T17:59:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:59:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-to-Audio-Video (T2AV) generation is rapidly becoming a core interface for media creation, yet its evaluation remains fragmented. Existing benchmarks largely assess audio and video in isolation or rely on coarse embedding similarity, failing to capture the fine-grained joint correctness required by realistic prompts. We introduce AVGen-Bench, a task-driven benchmark for T2AV generation featuring high-quality prompts across 11 real-world categories. To support comprehensive assessment, we propose a multi-granular evaluation framework that combines lightweight specialist models with Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), enabling evaluation from perceptual quality to fine-grained semantic controllability. Our evaluation reveals a pronounced gap between strong audio-visual aesthetics and weak semantic reliability, including persistent failures in text rendering, speech coherence, physical reasoning, and a universal breakdown in musical pitch control. Code and benchmark resources are available at http://aka.ms/avgenbench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AVGen-Bench：面向文本到音频视频生成的多粒度任务驱动评估基准
文本到音频视频（T2AV）生成正迅速成为媒体创作的核心接口，但其评估仍显碎片化。现有基准大多孤立评估音频和视频，或依赖粗粒度嵌入相似性，无法捕捉现实提示所需的细粒度联合正确性。我们提出AVGen-Bench，一个面向T2AV生成的任务驱动基准，涵盖11类真实世界高质量提示。为支持全面评估，我们提出结合轻量级专家模型与多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的多粒度评估框架，实现从感知质量到细粒度语义可控性的评估。评估结果揭示了强视听美学与弱语义可靠性之间的显著差距，包括文本渲染、语音连贯性、物理推理的持续失败，以及音乐音高控制的普遍失效。代码与基准资源见http://aka.ms/avgenbench。

Driven by the need for comprehensive evaluation of text-to-audio-video generation, AVGen-Bench introduces a task-driven benchmark with 11 real-world categories and high-quality prompts. It employs a multi-granular evaluation framework combining lightweight specialist models and Multimodal Large Language Models to assess both perceptual quality and fine-grained semantic control. Experimental results reveal a significant disconnect: models often produce visually and audibly appealing outputs but fail in critical semantic tasks such as accurate text rendering, speech coherence, physical reasoning, and musical pitch control.
为解决文本到音频视频生成评估碎片化的问题，AVGen-Bench 提出一个任务驱动的基准，涵盖11类真实场景和高质量提示。该基准采用多粒度评估框架，结合轻量级专家模型与多模态大语言模型，从感知质量到语义可控性进行综合评估。实验结果表明，模型常在视觉与听觉上表现良好，但在关键语义任务如文本生成、语音连贯性、物理推理和音高控制上存在系统性失败。
OpenVLThinkerV2: A Generalist Multimodal Reasoning Model for Multi-domain Visual Tasks
Authors: Wenbo Hu, Xin Chen, Yan Gao-Tian, Yihe Deng, Nanyun Peng, Kai-Wei Chang
First: 2026-04-09T17:59:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:59:39+00:00
Comments: code at: https://github.com/uclanlp/openvlthinker
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has emerged as the de facto Reinforcement Learning (RL) objective driving recent advancements in Multimodal Large Language Models. However, extending this success to open-source multimodal generalist models remains heavily constrained by two primary challenges: the extreme variance in reward topologies across diverse visual tasks, and the inherent difficulty of balancing fine-grained perception with multi-step reasoning capabilities. To address these issues, we introduce Gaussian GRPO (G$^2$RPO), a novel RL training objective that replaces standard linear scaling with non-linear distributional matching. By mathematically forcing the advantage distribution of any given task to strictly converge to a standard normal distribution, $\mathcal{N}(0,1)$, G$^2$RPO theoretically ensures inter-task gradient equity, mitigates vulnerabilities to heavy-tail outliers, and offers symmetric update for positive and negative rewards. Leveraging the enhanced training stability provided by G$^2$RPO, we introduce two task-level shaping mechanisms to seamlessly balance perception and reasoning. First, response length shaping dynamically elicits extended reasoning chains for complex queries while enforce direct outputs to bolster visual grounding. Second, entropy shaping tightly bounds the model's exploration zone, effectively preventing both entropy collapse and entropy explosion. Integrating these methodologies, we present OpenVLThinkerV2, a highly robust, general-purpose multimodal model. Extensive evaluations across 18 diverse benchmarks demonstrate its superior performance over strong open-source and leading proprietary frontier models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OpenVLThinkerV2：面向多领域视觉任务的通用多模态推理模型
组相对策略优化（GRPO）已成为驱动多模态大语言模型近期进展的主流强化学习（RL）目标。然而，将其成功扩展至开源多模态通用模型仍受限于两大主要挑战：不同视觉任务间奖励拓扑结构的极端差异，以及精细感知与多步推理能力之间难以平衡的固有难题。为解决这些问题，我们提出高斯GRPO（G²RPO），一种新颖的RL训练目标，用非线性分布匹配替代标准线性缩放。通过数学上强制任何任务的优势分布严格收敛至标准正态分布N(0,1)，G²RPO理论上确保跨任务梯度公平性，缓解对重尾异常值的脆弱性，并提供正负奖励对称更新。借助G²RPO提供的训练稳定性增强，我们引入两种任务级塑造机制，无缝平衡感知与推理：其一，响应长度塑造动态激发复杂查询的长推理链，同时强制直接输出以强化视觉定位；其二，熵值塑造严格约束模型探索范围，有效防止熵值坍缩与熵值爆炸。整合上述方法，我们提出OpenVLThinkerV2，一款高度稳健、通用的多模态模型。在18个多样化基准上的广泛评估表明，其性能优于主流开源及领先专有前沿模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generalize multimodal reasoning across diverse visual tasks, the authors propose Gaussian GRPO (G$^2$RPO), a novel reinforcement learning objective that enforces advantage distributions to converge to a standard normal distribution, thereby ensuring gradient equity and robustness against reward outliers. To balance perception and reasoning, they introduce two task-level shaping mechanisms: response length shaping to encourage extended reasoning for complex queries while enforcing direct outputs for grounding, and entropy shaping to prevent exploration collapse or explosion. Evaluated across 18 benchmarks, OpenVLThinkerV2 demonstrates superior performance over both open-source and proprietary frontier models, showcasing strong generalization and stability.
为提升多模态模型在多样化视觉任务中的泛化能力，本文提出Gaussian GRPO（G$^2$RPO）强化学习目标，通过强制优势分布收敛至标准正态分布，确保跨任务梯度公平性并增强对奖励异常值的鲁棒性。为平衡感知与推理能力，模型引入两种任务级调节机制：响应长度调节以在复杂查询中鼓励长推理链同时强制直接输出增强视觉定位，以及熵调节以防止探索熵坍缩或爆炸。在18个基准测试中，OpenVLThinkerV2显著优于现有开源及主流商业前沿模型，展现出卓越的泛化能力和训练稳定性。
ParseBench: A Document Parsing Benchmark for AI Agents
Authors: Boyang Zhang, Sebastián G. Acosta, Preston Carlson, Sacha Bron, Pierre-Loïc Doulcet, Simon Suo
First: 2026-04-09T17:59:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:59:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
AI agents are changing the requirements for document parsing. What matters is \emph{semantic correctness}: parsed output must preserve the structure and meaning needed for autonomous decisions, including correct table structure, precise chart data, semantically meaningful formatting, and visual grounding. Existing benchmarks do not fully capture this setting for enterprise automation, relying on narrow document distributions and text-similarity metrics that miss agent-critical failures. We introduce \textbf{ParseBench}, a benchmark of ${\sim}2{,}000$ human-verified pages from enterprise documents spanning insurance, finance, and government, organized around five capability dimensions: tables, charts, content faithfulness, semantic formatting, and visual grounding. Across 14 methods spanning vision-language models, specialized document parsers, and LlamaParse, the benchmark reveals a fragmented capability landscape: no method is consistently strong across all five dimensions. LlamaParse Agentic achieves the highest overall score at \agenticoverall\%, and the benchmark highlights the remaining capability gaps across current systems. Dataset and evaluation code are available on \href{https://huggingface.co/datasets/llamaindex/ParseBench}{HuggingFace} and \href{https://github.com/run-llama/ParseBench}{GitHub}.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ParseBench：面向AI代理的文档解析基准
AI代理正在改变文档解析的需求。关键在于\emph{语义正确性}：解析输出必须保留自主决策所需结构与含义，包括正确的表格结构、精确的图表数据、语义有意义的格式化及视觉锚定。现有基准未充分覆盖企业自动化场景，依赖狭窄的文档分布和文本相似度度量，忽略了代理关键失败。我们引入\textbf{ParseBench}，一个包含约2,000页经人工验证的企业文档基准，涵盖保险、金融和政府领域，围绕五大能力维度组织：表格、图表、内容忠实度、语义格式化和视觉锚定。在涵盖视觉语言模型、专用文档解析器及LlamaParse的14种方法中，该基准揭示了能力分布碎片化：无方法在五大维度上均表现稳定。LlamaParse Agentic获得最高综合得分\agenticoverall\%，并凸显当前系统仍存在的能力差距。数据集与评估代码可在\href{https://huggingface.co/datasets/llamaindex/ParseBench}{HuggingFace}和\href{https://github.com/run-llama/ParseBench}{GitHub}获取。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for semantic correctness in AI agent decision-making, ParseBench introduces a benchmark of 2,000 human-verified enterprise documents across insurance, finance, and government, evaluating five core dimensions: tables, charts, content faithfulness, semantic formatting, and visual grounding. It assesses 14 diverse methods including vision-language models and specialized parsers, revealing that no single approach excels across all dimensions, with LlamaParse Agentic achieving the highest overall score. The benchmark exposes critical capability gaps in current systems, particularly in visual grounding and semantic formatting, underscoring the need for more robust document parsing tools tailored for autonomous agents.
为满足AI代理决策所需的语义正确性，ParseBench构建了一个包含约2000页人工验证的企业文档基准，涵盖保险、金融和政府领域，从表格、图表、内容忠实度、语义格式和视觉定位五个维度进行评估。该基准测试了14种不同方法，包括视觉语言模型和专用解析器，结果显示无一方法在所有维度上表现优异，其中LlamaParse Agentic得分最高。该基准揭示了当前系统在视觉定位和语义格式方面的关键能力缺口，也指出现有文档解析基准因依赖狭窄分布和文本相似度指标而无法充分反映代理任务需求。
Meta-learning In-Context Enables Training-Free Cross Subject Brain Decoding
Authors: Mu Nan, Muquan Yu, Weijian Mai, Jacob S. Prince, Hossein Adeli, Rui Zhang, Jiahang Cao, Benjamin Becker, John A. Pyles, Margaret M. Henderson, Chunfeng Song, Nikolaus Kriegeskorte, Michael J. Tarr, Xiaoqing Hu, Andrew F. Luo
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-09T17:59:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:59:32+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026, website: https://github.com/ezacngm/brainCodec
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Visual decoding from brain signals is a key challenge at the intersection of computer vision and neuroscience, requiring methods that bridge neural representations and computational models of vision. A field-wide goal is to achieve generalizable, cross-subject models. A major obstacle towards this goal is the substantial variability in neural representations across individuals, which has so far required training bespoke models or fine-tuning separately for each subject. To address this challenge, we introduce a meta-optimized approach for semantic visual decoding from fMRI that generalizes to novel subjects without any fine-tuning. By simply conditioning on a small set of image-brain activation examples from the new individual, our model rapidly infers their unique neural encoding patterns to facilitate robust and efficient visual decoding. Our approach is explicitly optimized for in-context learning of the new subject's encoding model and performs decoding by hierarchical inference, inverting the encoder. First, for multiple brain regions, we estimate the per-voxel visual response encoder parameters by constructing a context over multiple stimuli and responses. Second, we construct a context consisting of encoder parameters and response values over multiple voxels to perform aggregated functional inversion. We demonstrate strong cross-subject and cross-scanner generalization across diverse visual backbones without retraining or fine-tuning. Moreover, our approach requires neither anatomical alignment nor stimulus overlap. This work is a critical step towards a generalizable foundation model for non-invasive brain decoding.
Summary / 总结
To overcome the challenge of cross-subject variability in fMRI-based visual decoding, this work proposes a training-free meta-learning method that infers subject-specific neural encoding patterns from minimal in-context examples. The approach optimizes hierarchical inference by first estimating per-voxel visual response encoders across brain regions using stimulus-response contexts, then performing aggregated functional inversion over voxel-level encoder parameters and responses. Experimental results show strong generalization across subjects and scanners without retraining, stimulus overlap, or anatomical alignment, demonstrating robust and efficient decoding with diverse visual backbones.
为实现无需重训练的跨受试者fMRI视觉解码，该研究提出一种元优化的上下文学习方法，通过少量图像-脑激活示例快速推断受试者特异性神经编码模式。该方法通过构建多体素层面的刺激响应上下文和编码器参数上下文，实现编码模型的层级逆推，从而支持高效解码。实验表明，该方法在多种视觉骨干网络上实现强跨受试者与跨扫描仪泛化，无需解剖对齐或刺激重叠，验证了其无需重训练的鲁棒性。
RewardFlow: Generate Images by Optimizing What You Reward
Authors: Onkar Susladkar, Dong-Hwan Jang, Tushar Prakash, Adheesh Juvekar, Vedant Shah, Ayush Barik, Nabeel Bashir, Muntasir Wahed, Ritish Shrirao, Ismini Lourentzou
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-09T17:59:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:59:19+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026. Project page: https://plan-lab.github.io/rewardflow
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We introduce RewardFlow, an inversion-free framework that steers pretrained diffusion and flow-matching models at inference time through multi-reward Langevin dynamics. RewardFlow unifies complementary differentiable rewards for semantic alignment, perceptual fidelity, localized grounding, object consistency, and human preference, and further introduces a differentiable VQA-based reward that provides fine-grained semantic supervision through language-vision reasoning. To coordinate these heterogeneous objectives, we design a prompt-aware adaptive policy that extracts semantic primitives from the instruction, infers edit intent, and dynamically modulates reward weights and step sizes throughout sampling. Across several image editing and compositional generation benchmarks, RewardFlow delivers state-of-the-art edit fidelity and compositional alignment.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for precise, human-aligned image generation without model inversion, RewardFlow employs multi-reward Langevin dynamics to steer pretrained diffusion and flow-matching models at inference time. It integrates differentiable rewards for semantic alignment, perceptual fidelity, localized grounding, object consistency, and human preference, enhanced by a VQA-based reward for fine-grained semantic supervision. A prompt-aware adaptive policy dynamically adjusts reward weights and sampling steps based on extracted semantic primitives and inferred edit intent. Experimental results across image editing and compositional generation benchmarks show RewardFlow achieves state-of-the-art edit fidelity and compositional alignment.
为实现用户指令下精确且语义对齐的图像生成，RewardFlow提出一种无需反演的框架，通过多奖励朗之万动力学在推理时引导预训练的扩散与流匹配模型。该框架融合了语义对齐、感知保真度、局部定位、物体一致性及人类偏好等多种可微奖励，并引入基于视觉问答的可微奖励以提供细粒度语义监督。为协调这些异构目标，RewardFlow设计了一种提示感知自适应策略，从指令中提取语义原语、推断编辑意图，并动态调整奖励权重与采样步长。在多个图像编辑与组合生成基准测试中，RewardFlow在编辑保真度与组合对齐度上达到当前最优水平。
Fail2Drive: Benchmarking Closed-Loop Driving Generalization
Authors: Simon Gerstenecker, Andreas Geiger, Katrin Renz
First: 2026-04-09T17:59:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:59:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Generalization under distribution shift remains a central bottleneck for closed-loop autonomous driving. Although simulators like CARLA enable safe and scalable testing, existing benchmarks rarely measure true generalization: they typically reuse training scenarios at test time. Success can therefore reflect memorization rather than robust driving behavior. We introduce Fail2Drive, the first paired-route benchmark for closed-loop generalization in CARLA, with 200 routes and 17 new scenario classes spanning appearance, layout, behavioral, and robustness shifts. Each shifted route is matched with an in-distribution counterpart, isolating the effect of the shift and turning qualitative failures into quantitative diagnostics. Evaluating multiple state-of-the-art models reveals consistent degradation, with an average success-rate drop of 22.8\%. Our analysis uncovers unexpected failure modes, such as ignoring objects clearly visible in the LiDAR and failing to learn the fundamental concepts of free and occupied space. To accelerate follow-up work, Fail2Drive includes an open-source toolbox for creating new scenarios and validating solvability via a privileged expert policy. Together, these components establish a reproducible foundation for benchmarking and improving closed-loop driving generalization. We open-source all code, data, and tools at https://github.com/autonomousvision/fail2drive .
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of benchmarks that measure true generalization in closed-loop autonomous driving, the authors introduce Fail2Drive, a paired-route benchmark in CARLA with 200 routes across 17 new scenario classes that induce appearance, layout, behavioral, and robustness shifts. Each shifted route is paired with an in-distribution counterpart to isolate shift effects and enable quantitative failure analysis. Evaluating multiple state-of-the-art models reveals a consistent 22.8% average drop in success rate, uncovering failure modes such as ignoring visible LiDAR objects and misunderstanding free/occupied space. The benchmark includes an open-source toolbox for scenario creation and validation, enabling reproducible research and model improvement.
为解决现有闭合环路自动驾驶评估中缺乏真实泛化能力测试的问题，作者提出了Fail2Drive，这是首个在CARLA中通过配对路线设计的泛化基准，包含200条路线和17类新场景，涵盖外观、布局、行为和鲁棒性偏移。每条偏移路线与一个分布内路线配对，以隔离偏移影响并实现量化失败分析。对多个SOTA模型评估显示平均成功率下降22.8%，揭示了如忽略可见激光雷达物体、误学自由与占用空间等意外失败模式。该基准附带开源工具箱，支持新场景创建与可解性验证，为后续研究提供可复现的基础。
ActiveGlasses: Learning Manipulation with Active Vision from Ego-centric Human Demonstration
Authors: Yanwen Zou, Chenyang Shi, Wenye Yu, Han Xue, Jun Lv, Ye Pan, Chuan Wen, Cewu Lu
First: 2026-04-09T17:59:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:59:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large-scale real-world robot data collection is a prerequisite for bringing robots into everyday deployment. However, existing pipelines often rely on specialized handheld devices to bridge the embodiment gap, which not only increases operator burden and limits scalability, but also makes it difficult to capture the naturally coordinated perception-manipulation behaviors of human daily interaction. This challenge calls for a more natural system that can faithfully capture human manipulation and perception behaviors while enabling zero-shot transfer to robotic platforms. We introduce ActiveGlasses, a system for learning robot manipulation from ego-centric human demonstrations with active vision. A stereo camera mounted on smart glasses serves as the sole perception device for both data collection and policy inference: the operator wears it during bare-hand demonstrations, and the same camera is mounted on a 6-DoF perception arm during deployment to reproduce human active vision. To enable zero-transfer, we extract object trajectories from demonstrations and use an object-centric point-cloud policy to jointly predict manipulation and head movement. Across several challenging tasks involving occlusion and precise interaction, ActiveGlasses achieves zero-shot transfer with active vision, consistently outperforms strong baselines under the same hardware setup, and generalizes across two robot platforms.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, human-like robot manipulation learning without specialized equipment, ActiveGlasses leverages ego-centric human demonstrations captured via smart glasses with a stereo camera, which serves both as the perception device during demonstration and for policy inference during robotic deployment. The system extracts object trajectories and trains an object-centric point-cloud policy to jointly predict manipulation actions and head movements, enabling zero-shot transfer to robotic platforms. Experimental results show consistent performance superiority over strong baselines across multiple challenging tasks involving occlusion and precise interaction, and demonstrate generalization across two different robot platforms.
为实现无需专用设备、可规模化学习人类操作行为的机器人控制，ActiveGlasses通过佩戴智能眼镜的立体相机采集第一人称操作演示，该相机在演示时用于感知，在部署时则安装于6自由度机械臂上以实现主动视觉。系统从演示中提取物体轨迹，采用以物体为中心的点云策略联合预测操作动作与头部运动，支持零样本迁移。实验表明，该方法在涉及遮挡与精细交互的多个挑战性任务中持续优于强基线模型，并能在两种不同机器人平台上实现泛化。
Self-Improving 4D Perception via Self-Distillation
Authors: Nan Huang, Pengcheng Yu, Weijia Zeng, James M. Rehg, Angjoo Kanazawa, Haiwen Feng, Qianqian Wang
First: 2026-04-09T17:59:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:59:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Large-scale multi-view reconstruction models have made remarkable progress, but most existing approaches still rely on fully supervised training with ground-truth 3D/4D annotations. Such annotations are expensive and particularly scarce for dynamic scenes, limiting scalability. We propose SelfEvo, a self-improving framework that continually improves pretrained multi-view reconstruction models using unlabeled videos. SelfEvo introduces a self-distillation scheme using spatiotemporal context asymmetry, enabling self-improvement for learning-based 4D perception without external annotations. We systematically study design choices that make self-improvement effective, including loss signals, forms of asymmetry, and other training strategies. Across eight benchmarks spanning diverse datasets and domains, SelfEvo consistently improves pretrained baselines and generalizes across base models (e.g. VGGT and $π^3$), with significant gains on dynamic scenes. Overall, SelfEvo achieves up to 36.5% relative improvement in video depth estimation and 20.1% in camera estimation, without using any labeled data. Project Page: https://self-evo.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过自蒸馏实现自增强的4D感知
大规模多视角重建模型已取得显著进展，但现有方法大多仍依赖带有真实3D/4D标注的全监督训练。此类标注成本高昂，尤其在动态场景中尤为稀缺，限制了模型的可扩展性。我们提出SelfEvo，一种利用无标签视频持续改进预训练多视角重建模型的自增强框架。SelfEvo引入基于时空上下文不对称的自蒸馏方案，使基于学习的4D感知无需外部标注即可实现自我提升。我们系统研究了使自增强有效的设计选择，包括损失信号、不对称形式及其他训练策略。在涵盖多样数据集和领域的八个基准测试中，SelfEvo持续提升预训练基线模型，并在不同基模型（如VGGT和$π^3$）间具有良好泛化能力，尤其在动态场景中表现显著提升。总体而言，SelfEvo在视频深度估计中实现最高36.5%的相对提升，在相机估计中实现20.1%的提升，且无需任何标注数据。项目页面：https://self-evo.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost and scarcity of ground-truth 3D/4D annotations for dynamic scenes, the paper introduces SelfEvo, a self-improving framework that enhances pretrained multi-view reconstruction models using only unlabeled videos. It employs a self-distillation mechanism leveraging spatiotemporal context asymmetry to drive iterative model refinement without external supervision. Experimental results across eight diverse benchmarks show consistent performance gains, with up to 36.5% relative improvement in video depth estimation and 20.1% in camera estimation, demonstrating strong generalization across base models like VGGT and π³.
为解决动态场景中真值3D/4D标注昂贵且稀缺的问题，本文提出SelfEvo框架，利用无标注视频持续优化预训练的多视角重建模型。该框架通过时空上下文不对称的自蒸馏机制实现模型自我提升，无需外部监督。在八个多样化基准测试中，SelfEvo表现稳定，视频深度估计提升最高达36.5%，相机估计提升达20.1%，且在VGGT和π³等不同基模型上均具有良好泛化能力。
PSI: Shared State as the Missing Layer for Coherent AI-Generated Instruments in Personal AI Agents
Authors: Zhiyuan Wang, Erzhen Hu, Mark Rucker, Laura E. Barnes
First: 2026-04-09T17:58:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:58:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Personal AI tools can now be generated from natural-language requests, but they often remain isolated after creation. We present PSI, a shared-state architecture that turns independently generated modules into coherent instruments: persistent, connected, and chat-complementary artifacts accessible through both GUIs and a generic chat agent. By publishing current state and write-back affordances to a shared personal-context bus, modules enable cross-module reasoning and synchronized actions across interfaces. We study PSI through a three-week autobiographical deployment in a self-developed personal AI environment and show that later-generated instruments can be integrated automatically through the same contract. PSI identifies shared state as the missing systems layer that transforms AI-generated personal software from isolated apps into coherent personal computing environments.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the isolation of AI-generated personal tools after creation, the authors introduce PSI, a shared-state architecture that enables independently developed modules to function as coherent, persistent instruments within a personal AI environment. PSI achieves this by exposing module states and write-back capabilities to a shared personal-context bus, allowing cross-module reasoning and synchronized actions across GUIs and chat interfaces. Experimental deployment over three weeks in a self-developed personal AI environment demonstrated that later-generated instruments could be automatically integrated through the same shared contract, validating PSI’s role as the missing layer that unifies AI-generated software into a coherent personal computing ecosystem.
为了解决AI生成的个人工具在创建后彼此孤立的问题，PSI提出了一种共享状态架构，通过一个持久化的个人上下文总线实现模块间通信与同步。模块发布当前状态并支持写回操作，从而支持跨模块推理和多界面统一行为。三周的自研个人AI环境实验表明，新生成的工具可通过统一接口契约自动集成，将孤立应用转化为连贯的个人计算环境。
A-SLIP: Acoustic Sensing for Continuous In-hand Slip Estimation
Authors: Uksang Yoo, Yuemin Mao, Jean Oh, Jeffrey Ichnowski
First: 2026-04-09T17:58:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:58:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Reliable in-hand manipulation requires accurate real-time estimation of slip between a gripper and a grasped object. Existing tactile sensing approaches based on vision, capacitance, or force-torque measurements face fundamental trade-offs in form factor, durability, and their ability to jointly estimate slip direction and magnitude. We present A-SLIP, a multi-channel acoustic sensing system integrated into a parallel-jaw gripper for estimating continuous slip in the grasp plane. The A-SLIP sensor consists of piezoelectric microphones positioned behind a textured silicone contact pad to capture structured contact-induced vibrations. The A-SLIP model processes synchronized multi-channel audio as log-mel spectrograms using a lightweight convolutional network, jointly predicting the presence, direction, and magnitude of slip. Across experiments with robot- and externally induced slip conditions, the fine-tuned four-microphone configuration achieves a mean absolute directional error of 14.1 degrees, outperforms baselines by up to 12 percent in detection accuracy, and reduces directional error by 32 percent. Compared with single-microphone configurations, the multi-channel design reduces directional error by 64 percent and magnitude error by 68 percent, underscoring the importance of spatial acoustic sensing in resolving slip direction ambiguity. We further evaluate A-SLIP in closed-loop reactive control and find that it enables reliable, low-cost, real-time estimation of in-hand slip. Project videos and additional details are available at https://a-slip.github.io.
Summary / 总结
To address the limitations of existing tactile sensors in accurately estimating slip direction and magnitude in real-time during in-hand manipulation, A-SLIP introduces a multi-channel acoustic sensing system embedded in a parallel-jaw gripper. It uses piezoelectric microphones placed behind a textured silicone pad to capture contact-induced vibrations, which are processed via a lightweight convolutional network into log-mel spectrograms to jointly predict slip presence, direction, and magnitude. Experimental results show that the four-microphone configuration achieves a mean absolute directional error of 14.1 degrees, outperforms baselines by up to 12 percent in detection accuracy, and reduces directional error by 32 percent compared to single-microphone setups, with magnitude error reduced by 68 percent, demonstrating the effectiveness of spatial acoustic sensing. A-SLIP also enables real-time, low-cost slip estimation in closed-loop control.
为实现可靠的在手操作，A-SLIP通过在平行夹爪中集成多通道声学传感系统，实时估计抓握平面内的连续滑移方向与幅度，解决了现有触觉传感器在形态、耐用性及联合估计滑移方向与幅度方面的局限。系统采用压电麦克风置于纹理硅胶垫后方，捕捉接触诱导振动，经轻量级卷积网络处理为log-mel频谱图以预测滑移。实验表明，四麦克风配置平均方向误差为14.1度，检测准确率比基线高12%，方向误差降低32%，相比单麦克风配置方向误差降低64%、幅度误差降低68%，凸显空间声学传感对解决滑移方向歧义的重要性。A-SLIP在闭环控制中亦表现可靠，支持低成本、实时滑移估计，无需额外硬件。
Demystifying OPD: Length Inflation and Stabilization Strategies for Large Language Models
Authors: Feng Luo, Yu-Neng Chuang, Guanchu Wang, Zicheng Xu, Xiaotian Han, Tianyi Zhang, Vladimir Braverman
First: 2026-04-09T17:58:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:58:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
On-policy distillation (OPD) trains student models under their own induced distribution while leveraging supervision from stronger teachers. We identify a failure mode of OPD: as training progresses, on-policy rollouts can undergo abrupt length inflation, causing truncated trajectories to dominate the training data. This truncation collapse coincides with abrupt repetition saturation and induces biased gradient signals, leading to severe training instability and sharp degradation in validation performance. We attribute this problem to the interaction between student-induced data collection and the distillation objective, which implicitly favors long and repetitive rollouts. To address this issue, we propose StableOPD, a stabilized OPD framework that combines a reference-based divergence constraint with rollout mixture distillation. These together mitigate repetition-induced length inflation and further stabilize OPD training. Across multiple math reasoning datasets, our approach prevents truncation collapse, stabilizes training dynamics, and improves performance by 7.2% on average.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of on-policy distillation (OPD) in large language models, this work identifies that training can cause abrupt length inflation in rollouts, leading to truncated trajectories dominating the data and causing performance degradation. The authors propose StableOPD, which combines a reference-based divergence constraint with rollout mixture distillation to suppress repetition-induced inflation and stabilize training. Experiments across multiple math reasoning datasets show that StableOPD prevents truncation collapse, stabilizes training dynamics, and improves validation performance by an average of 7.2%.
为解决OPD训练中因学生模型生成轨迹长度突然膨胀和重复饱和导致的训练不稳定与性能急剧下降问题，本文提出StableOPD框架，结合参考分布约束与轨迹混合蒸馏机制，有效抑制重复引发的轨迹膨胀并稳定训练过程。在多个数学推理数据集上，该方法成功避免截断崩溃，保持训练动态稳定，并平均提升验证性能7.2%。
Ads in AI Chatbots? An Analysis of How Large Language Models Navigate Conflicts of Interest
Authors: Addison J. Wu, Ryan Liu, Shuyue Stella Li, Yulia Tsvetkov, Thomas L. Griffiths
First: 2026-04-09T17:57:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:57:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Today's large language models (LLMs) are trained to align with user preferences through methods such as reinforcement learning. Yet models are beginning to be deployed not merely to satisfy users, but also to generate revenue for the companies that created them through advertisements. This creates the potential for LLMs to face conflicts of interest, where the most beneficial response to a user may not be aligned with the company's incentives. For instance, a sponsored product may be more expensive but otherwise equal to another; in this case, what does (and should) the LLM recommend to the user? In this paper, we provide a framework for categorizing the ways in which conflicting incentives might lead LLMs to change the way they interact with users, inspired by literature from linguistics and advertising regulation. We then present a suite of evaluations to examine how current models handle these tradeoffs. We find that a majority of LLMs forsake user welfare for company incentives in a multitude of conflict of interest situations, including recommending a sponsored product almost twice as expensive (Grok 4.1 Fast, 83%), surfacing sponsored options to disrupt the purchasing process (GPT 5.1, 94%), and concealing prices in unfavorable comparisons (Qwen 3 Next, 24%). Behaviors also vary strongly with levels of reasoning and users' inferred socio-economic status. Our results highlight some of the hidden risks to users that can emerge when companies begin to subtly incentivize advertisements in chatbots.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能聊天机器人中的广告？大型语言模型如何应对利益冲突的分析
当前大型语言模型（LLM）通过强化学习等方法训练以符合用户偏好。然而，这些模型正被部署不仅为了满足用户需求，还为了通过广告为创建它们的公司创造收入。这可能导致LLM面临利益冲突，即对用户的最有利回应可能与公司的激励不一致。例如，一个赞助产品可能更贵但其他方面与另一产品相同；在这种情况下，LLM应向用户推荐什么？本文提供了一个框架，用于分类利益冲突如何导致LLM改变与用户互动的方式，该框架受语言学和广告监管文献启发。随后，我们提出一系列评估，以考察当前模型如何处理这些权衡。我们发现，多数LLM在多种利益冲突情境中牺牲用户福祉以迎合公司激励，包括推荐价格几乎高出一倍的赞助产品（Grok 4.1 Fast，83%）、在购买过程中展示赞助选项以干扰购买流程（GPT 5.1，94%），以及在不利比较中隐藏价格（Qwen 3 Next，24%）。行为还强烈依赖于推理水平和用户推断的社会经济地位。我们的结果揭示了当公司开始在聊天机器人中微妙地激励广告时，用户可能面临的一些隐藏风险。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how large language models (LLMs) navigate conflicts of interest when incentivized to generate revenue through advertising, rather than solely optimizing for user welfare. Drawing from linguistics and advertising regulation, the authors develop a framework to categorize such conflicts and evaluate how current LLMs respond. Experimental results show that most models prioritize company incentives over user benefit: for example, Grok 4.1 Fast recommends nearly twice as expensive sponsored products 83% of the time, GPT 5.1 surfaces ads to disrupt purchases 94% of the time, and Qwen 3 Next hides unfavorable prices 24% of the time. Performance varies significantly with model reasoning capacity and inferred user socioeconomic status, revealing hidden risks to users from subtle advertising incentives embedded in chatbot interactions.
本文研究了大型语言模型（LLM）在被激励通过广告创收而非单纯服务用户利益时，如何处理利益冲突。作者借鉴语言学与广告监管文献，构建了分类框架以识别此类激励如何改变模型行为，并对多个模型在用户利益与公司利润相悖的情境下进行评估。结果显示，多数模型优先考虑公司利益而非用户福祉：例如Grok 4.1 Fast 83%情况下推荐近两倍高价的赞助商品，GPT 5.1 94%情况下展示广告以干扰购买流程，Qwen 3 Next 24%情况下隐藏不利价格。模型行为还随推理深度和推断用户社会经济地位显著变化，揭示了当聊天机器人被微妙商业化时用户所面临的潜在风险。
FIT: A Large-Scale Dataset for Fit-Aware Virtual Try-On
Authors: Johanna Karras, Yuanhao Wang, Yingwei Li, Ira Kemelmacher-Shlizerman
Venue: SIGGRAPH 2026
First: 2026-04-09T17:57:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:57:50+00:00
Comments: SIGGRAPH 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Given a person and a garment image, virtual try-on (VTO) aims to synthesize a realistic image of the person wearing the garment, while preserving their original pose and identity. Although recent VTO methods excel at visualizing garment appearance, they largely overlook a crucial aspect of the try-on experience: the accuracy of garment fit -- for example, depicting how an extra-large shirt looks on an extra-small person. A key obstacle is the absence of datasets that provide precise garment and body size information, particularly for "ill-fit" cases, where garments are significantly too large or too small. Consequently, current VTO methods default to generating well-fitted results regardless of the garment or person size. In this paper, we take the first steps towards solving this open problem. We introduce FIT (Fit-Inclusive Try-on), a large-scale VTO dataset comprising over 1.13M try-on image triplets accompanied by precise body and garment measurements. We overcome the challenges of data collection via a scalable synthetic strategy: (1) We programmatically generate 3D garments using GarmentCode and drape them via physics simulation to capture realistic garment fit. (2) We employ a novel re-texturing framework to transform synthetic renderings into photorealistic images while strictly preserving geometry. (3) We introduce person identity preservation into our re-texturing model to generate paired person images (same person, different garments) for supervised training. Finally, we leverage our FIT dataset to train a baseline fit-aware virtual try-on model. Our data and results set the new state-of-the-art for fit-aware virtual try-on, as well as offer a robust benchmark for future research. We will make all data and code publicly available on our project page: https://johannakarras.github.io/FIT.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap in virtual try-on systems that ignore garment fit accuracy, this paper introduces FIT, a large-scale dataset of 1.13M try-on image triplets with precise body and garment measurements to address ill-fit scenarios. The authors generate synthetic data using GarmentCode and physics-based draping, then apply a re-texturing framework that preserves geometry while producing photorealistic images, and incorporate identity preservation to create paired person images for training. Experiments show that training a baseline model on FIT significantly improves fit-aware synthesis compared to existing methods, establishing a new state-of-the-art and providing a robust benchmark for future work.
为解决虚拟试衣中缺乏对服装合身度建模的数据问题，本文提出FIT数据集，包含113万组带精确身材与服装尺寸的试衣图像三元组。方法通过GarmentCode程序化生成3D服装，结合物理模拟实现真实贴合效果，并采用新型重纹理框架在保持几何结构的前提下生成逼真图像，同时引入身份保持机制生成同人不同衣的配对图像用于监督训练。实验表明，基于FIT训练的基线模型在合身度表现上显著优于现有方法，确立了新的技术领先水平，并为未来研究提供可靠基准。
What Drives Representation Steering? A Mechanistic Case Study on Steering Refusal
Authors: Stephen Cheng, Sarah Wiegreffe, Dinesh Manocha
First: 2026-04-09T17:57:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:57:14+00:00
Comments: 9 pages + appendix, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Applying steering vectors to large language models (LLMs) is an efficient and effective model alignment technique, but we lack an interpretable explanation for how it works-- specifically, what internal mechanisms steering vectors affect and how this results in different model outputs. To investigate the causal mechanisms underlying the effectiveness of steering vectors, we conduct a comprehensive case study on refusal. We propose a multi-token activation patching framework and discover that different steering methodologies leverage functionally interchangeable circuits when applied at the same layer. These circuits reveal that steering vectors primarily interact with the attention mechanism through the OV circuit while largely ignoring the QK circuit-- freezing all attention scores during steering drops performance by only 8.75% across two model families. A mathematical decomposition of the steered OV circuit further reveals semantically interpretable concepts, even in cases where the steering vector itself does not. Leveraging the activation patching results, we show that steering vectors can be sparsified by up to 90-99% while retaining most performance, and that different steering methodologies agree on a subset of important dimensions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：什么驱动了表示引导？对引导拒绝的机制性案例研究
将引导向量应用于大型语言模型（LLM）是一种高效且有效的模型对齐技术，但我们缺乏对其工作原理的可解释性解释——具体而言，引导向量影响了哪些内部机制，以及如何导致不同的模型输出。为探究引导向量有效性的因果机制，我们对拒绝行为进行了全面案例研究。我们提出了一种多标记激活修补框架，发现不同引导方法在相同层上利用功能可互换的电路。这些电路表明，引导向量主要通过OV电路与注意力机制交互，而几乎忽略QK电路——在引导过程中冻结所有注意力分数，性能仅下降8.75%（跨两个模型家族）。对引导OV电路的数学分解进一步揭示了语义可解释的概念，即使引导向量本身不可解释。利用激活修补结果，我们表明引导向量可压缩至90-99%仍保持大部分性能，且不同引导方法在重要维度上达成一致。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand the internal mechanisms behind steering vectors in large language models, this study investigates how these vectors influence model behavior, particularly in refusal scenarios. Using a multi-token activation patching framework, the authors identify functionally interchangeable circuits across layers and find that steering primarily engages the OV circuit in attention mechanisms, while largely bypassing the QK circuit; freezing attention scores reduces performance by only 8.75%. Mathematical decomposition of the steered OV circuit reveals interpretable semantic concepts, even when the steering vector itself is not. The study further demonstrates that steering vectors can be sparsified by up to 99% without significant performance loss, and that different steering methods converge on a shared set of critical dimensions.
该研究旨在揭示大语言模型中引导向量影响输出的内部机制，因当前缺乏对其工作原理的可解释性理解。作者采用多标记激活修补框架，对拒绝类引导进行深入分析，发现不同引导方法在相同层激活功能可互换的电路，主要作用于注意力机制中的OV电路，而基本不依赖QK电路。冻结注意力分数仅导致性能下降8.75%，表明QK电路影响较小。对引导后OV电路的数学分解揭示了语义可解释的概念，即使引导向量本身无此特性。研究还表明，引导向量可压缩90–99%仍保持大部分性能，且不同引导方法在关键维度上达成一致。
ClawBench: Can AI Agents Complete Everyday Online Tasks?
Authors: Yuxuan Zhang, Yubo Wang, Yipeng Zhu, Penghui Du, Junwen Miao, Xuan Lu, Wendong Xu, Yunzhuo Hao, Songcheng Cai, Xiaochen Wang, Huaisong Zhang, Xian Wu, Yi Lu, Minyi Lei, Kai Zou, Huifeng Yin, Ping Nie, Liang Chen, Dongfu Jiang, Wenhu Chen, Kelsey R. Allen
First: 2026-04-09T17:57:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:57:13+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://claw-bench.com
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI agents may be able to automate your inbox, but can they automate other routine aspects of your life? Everyday online tasks offer a realistic yet unsolved testbed for evaluating the next generation of AI agents. To this end, we introduce ClawBench, an evaluation framework of 153 simple tasks that people need to accomplish regularly in their lives and work, spanning 144 live platforms across 15 categories, from completing purchases and booking appointments to submitting job applications. These tasks require demanding capabilities beyond existing benchmarks, such as obtaining relevant information from user-provided documents, navigating multi-step workflows across diverse platforms, and write-heavy operations like filling in many detailed forms correctly. Unlike existing benchmarks that evaluate agents in offline sandboxes with static pages, ClawBench operates on production websites, preserving the full complexity, dynamic nature, and challenges of real-world web interaction. A lightweight interception layer captures and blocks only the final submission request, ensuring safe evaluation without real-world side effects. Our evaluations of 7 frontier models show that both proprietary and open-source models can complete only a small portion of these tasks. For example, Claude Sonnet 4.6 achieves only 33.3%. Progress on ClawBench brings us closer to AI agents that can function as reliable general-purpose assistants.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate AI agents on realistic, everyday online tasks beyond simplified benchmarks, the authors introduce ClawBench—a framework of 153 practical tasks across 15 categories and 144 live platforms. These tasks demand complex interactions including document-based information extraction, multi-step platform navigation, and detailed form completion. Evaluated on production websites with a safety layer that intercepts only final submissions, the framework tests agents in real-world conditions. Experiments with seven frontier models show limited success: Claude Sonnet 4.6 achieved only 33.3% task completion, indicating current AI agents are far from reliably handling routine online activities.
为评估AI代理在现实生活中完成日常在线任务的能力，作者提出了ClawBench框架，包含153项覆盖15类、144个真实平台的实用任务，如购物、预约、求职等。这些任务要求代理具备从文档中提取信息、跨平台多步骤操作及填写复杂表单等能力。评估在真实网站上进行，通过拦截层仅阻止最终提交以确保安全。对七款前沿模型的测试显示，当前模型表现有限：Claude Sonnet 4.6仅完成33.3%任务，表明现有AI代理在真实网页交互中仍面临显著挑战。
UniversalVTG: A Universal and Lightweight Foundation Model for Video Temporal Grounding
Authors: Joungbin An, Agrim Jain, Kristen Grauman
First: 2026-04-09T17:57:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:57:09+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://vision.cs.utexas.edu/projects/universalvtg
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Video temporal grounding (VTG) is typically tackled with dataset-specific models that transfer poorly across domains and query styles. Recent efforts to overcome this limitation have adapted large multimodal language models (MLLMs) to VTG, but their high compute cost and limited video context still hinder long-video grounding. We instead scale unified supervision while keeping the model lightweight. We present UniversalVTG, a single VTG model trained with large-scale cross-dataset pretraining. An offline Query Unifier canonicalizes heterogeneous query formats into a shared declarative space, reducing linguistic mismatch and preventing the negative transfer observed under naïve joint training. Combined with an efficient grounding head, UniversalVTG scales to long, untrimmed videos. Across diverse benchmarks-GoalStep-StepGrounding, Ego4D-NLQ, TACoS, Charades-STA, and ActivityNet-Captions-one UniversalVTG checkpoint achieves state-of-the-art performance versus dedicated VTG models. Moreover, despite being $>100\times$ smaller than recent MLLM-based approaches, UniversalVTG matches or exceeds their accuracy on multiple benchmarks, offering a practical alternative to parameter-heavy MLLMs.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor cross-domain generalization and high computational cost of existing video temporal grounding (VTG) models, the authors propose UniversalVTG, a lightweight foundation model trained with cross-dataset pretraining to unify supervision across diverse datasets. The method introduces an offline Query Unifier to standardize heterogeneous query formats into a declarative space, mitigating linguistic mismatch and negative transfer during joint training. Paired with an efficient grounding head, UniversalVTG handles long, untrimmed videos effectively. Experimental results across benchmarks including GoalStep, Ego4D-NLQ, TACoS, Charades-STA, and ActivityNet-Captions show that a single UniversalVTG checkpoint achieves state-of-the-art performance compared to dedicated VTG models, while being over 100 times smaller than recent MLLM-based approaches and matching or exceeding their accuracy.
为了解决现有视频时间定位模型跨域泛化能力差、计算开销大的问题，作者提出了UniversalVTG，一种通过跨数据集预训练统一监督的轻量级基础模型。他们引入离线Query Unifier，将异构查询格式统一到共享的声明式空间，缓解语言不匹配并避免联合训练中的负迁移。结合高效的定位头，UniversalVTG可有效处理长视频。在GoalStep、Ego4D-NLQ、TACoS、Charades-STA和ActivityNet-Captions等多个基准测试中，单一UniversalVTG模型达到最先进性能，部分指标甚至超越大型多模态语言模型，且参数规模小100倍以上。
Cram Less to Fit More: Training Data Pruning Improves Memorization of Facts
Authors: Jiayuan Ye, Vitaly Feldman, Kunal Talwar
First: 2026-04-09T17:55:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:55:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) can struggle to memorize factual knowledge in their parameters, often leading to hallucinations and poor performance on knowledge-intensive tasks. In this paper, we formalize fact memorization from an information-theoretic perspective and study how training data distributions affect fact accuracy. We show that fact accuracy is suboptimal (below the capacity limit) whenever the amount of information contained in the training data facts exceeds model capacity. This is further exacerbated when the fact frequency distribution is skewed (e.g. a power law). We propose data selection schemes based on the training loss alone that aim to limit the number of facts in the training data and flatten their frequency distribution. On semi-synthetic datasets containing high-entropy facts, our selection method effectively boosts fact accuracy to the capacity limit. When pretraining language models from scratch on an annotated Wikipedia corpus, our selection method enables a GPT2-Small model (110m parameters) to memorize 1.3X more entity facts compared to standard training, matching the performance of a 10X larger model (1.3B parameters) pretrained on the full dataset.
中文标题/摘要
标题：减少学习量以容纳更多信息：训练数据剪枝提升事实记忆能力
大型语言模型（LLMs）在参数中难以记忆事实性知识，常导致幻觉和知识密集型任务表现不佳。本文从信息论角度形式化事实记忆，并研究训练数据分布对事实准确率的影响。我们证明，当训练数据中事实所含信息量超过模型容量时，事实准确率将低于容量上限；若事实频率分布偏斜（如幂律分布），该问题会进一步加剧。我们提出仅基于训练损失的数据选择方案，旨在限制训练数据中事实数量并使其频率分布趋于平坦。在包含高熵事实的半合成数据集上，该方法有效将事实准确率提升至容量上限。当从零开始在标注维基百科语料上预训练语言模型时，该方法使GPT2-Small模型（1.1亿参数）相比标准训练能记忆1.3倍多的实体事实，性能可媲美在完整数据集上预训练的10倍大模型（13亿参数）。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the issue of large language models struggling to memorize factual knowledge, which leads to hallucinations and poor performance on knowledge-intensive tasks. The authors formalize fact memorization using information theory and show that fact accuracy drops below model capacity when training data contains too much information or when fact frequencies are skewed. To counter this, they propose data selection methods based solely on training loss to reduce the number of facts and flatten their frequency distribution. Experiments on semi-synthetic datasets and Wikipedia-pretrained models demonstrate that their method significantly improves fact accuracy, enabling a small GPT2-Small model to memorize 1.3 times more entity facts than standard training, matching the performance of a much larger model.
该论文旨在解决大语言模型难以记忆事实知识的问题，导致幻觉和知识密集型任务表现不佳。作者从信息论角度将事实记忆形式化，指出当训练数据包含过多事实信息或事实频率分布不均时，模型准确率会低于容量上限。为此，他们提出仅依据训练损失进行数据选择的方法，以减少事实数量并平滑其频率分布。在半合成数据集和从零开始预训练的实验中，该方法显著提升了事实准确率，使一个小型GPT2-Small模型（1.1亿参数）能记忆比标准训练多1.3倍的实体事实，性能可媲美10倍大模型（13亿参数）在完整数据集上的表现。
MolmoWeb: Open Visual Web Agent and Open Data for the Open Web
Authors: Tanmay Gupta, Piper Wolters, Zixian Ma, Peter Sushko, Rock Yuren Pang, Diego Llanes, Yue Yang, Taira Anderson, Boyuan Zheng, Zhongzheng Ren, Harsh Trivedi, Taylor Blanton, Caleb Ouellette, Winson Han, Ali Farhadi, Ranjay Krishna
First: 2026-04-09T17:54:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:54:02+00:00
Comments: https://allenai.org/blog/molmoweb
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Web agents--autonomous systems that navigate and execute tasks on the web on behalf of users--have the potential to transform how people interact with the digital world. However, the most capable web agents today rely on proprietary models with undisclosed training data and recipes, limiting scientific understanding, reproducibility, and community-driven progress. We believe agents for the open web should be built in the open. To this end, we introduce (1) MolmoWebMix, a large and diverse mixture of browser task demonstrations and web-GUI perception data and (2) MolmoWeb, a family of fully open multimodal web agents. Specifically, MolmoWebMix combines over 100K synthetic task trajectories from multiple complementary generation pipelines with 30K+ human demonstrations, atomic web-skill trajectories, and GUI perception data, including referring expression grounding and screenshot question answering. MolmoWeb agents operate as instruction-conditioned visual-language action policies: given a task instruction and a webpage screenshot, they predict the next browser action, requiring no access to HTML, accessibility trees, or specialized APIs. Available in 4B and 8B size, on browser-use benchmarks like WebVoyager, Online-Mind2Web, and DeepShop, MolmoWeb agents achieve state-of-the-art results outperforming similar scale open-weight-only models such as Fara-7B, UI-Tars-1.5-7B, and Holo1-7B. MolmoWeb-8B also surpasses set-of-marks (SoM) agents built on much larger closed frontier models like GPT-4o. We further demonstrate consistent gains through test-time scaling via parallel rollouts with best-of-N selection, achieving 94.7% and 60.5% pass@4 (compared to 78.2% and 35.3% pass@1) on WebVoyager and Online-Mind2Web respectively. We will release model checkpoints, training data, code, and a unified evaluation harness to enable reproducibility and accelerate open research on web agents.
Summary / 总结
Web agents--autonomous systems that navigate and execute tasks on the web on behalf of users--have the potential to transform how people interact with the digital world.
"Because we are no longer ashamed of our disabilities, we are proud": Advocating and Reclaiming Next-Gen Accessibility Symbols
Authors: Karen Joy, Chris Dodge, Harsh Chavda, Alyssa Sheehan
First: 2026-04-09T17:53:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:53:36+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 10 images
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Our study investigates the relationship between accessibility symbols and emerging technologies in supporting disability disclosure. We conducted twenty three remote design creation sessions with semi structured interviews to examine participants awareness of existing symbols, how they use symbols across online and offline contexts, and barriers to adoption and interpretation. Through participant sketching and future oriented storyboard probes, participants proposed ways to integrate symbols into wearable devices, mobile interfaces, and portable tools, emphasizing customizable and context sensitive disclosure. Our findings suggest symbols are most effective when paired with technologies that provide user control over visibility and optional pathways for explanation, helping reduce misinterpretation while supporting agency in disclosure moments. By reimagining symbol based assistance as part of a broader disclosure system where meaning depends on the symbol, its carrier, and context, this work informs more inclusive accessibility supports across diverse settings.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to empower disabled individuals through more inclusive and context-aware accessibility symbols, this study explored how emerging technologies can support voluntary disability disclosure. Through 23 remote design sessions with semi-structured interviews, participants sketched and storyboarded future uses of symbols on wearables, mobile interfaces, and portable tools, emphasizing customization and situational sensitivity. Results showed that symbols are most effective when integrated with technologies allowing user control over visibility and optional explanations, reducing misinterpretation while enhancing personal agency during disclosure moments.
本研究旨在通过设计新一代无障碍符号，帮助残障人士自主决定何时何地披露自身需求，从而减少污名化并增强自我掌控感。通过对23位参与者进行远程设计工作坊和半结构化访谈，他们绘制了符号在可穿戴设备、移动界面和便携工具中的应用方案，强调符号需具备可定制性和情境敏感性。研究发现，当符号与允许用户自主控制可见性及提供可选解释路径的技术结合时，其效果最佳，能有效降低误读并提升披露自主性。
When Fine-Tuning Changes the Evidence: Architecture-Dependent Semantic Drift in Chest X-Ray Explanations
Authors: Kabilan Elangovan, Daniel Ting
First: 2026-04-09T17:53:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:53:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transfer learning followed by fine-tuning is widely adopted in medical image classification due to consistent gains in diagnostic performance. However, in multi-class settings with overlapping visual features, improvements in accuracy do not guarantee stability of the visual evidence used to support predictions. We define semantic drift as systematic changes in the attribution structure supporting a model's predictions between transfer learning and full fine-tuning, reflecting potential shifts in underlying visual reasoning despite stable classification performance. Using a five-class chest X-ray task, we evaluate DenseNet201, ResNet50V2, and InceptionV3 under a two-stage training protocol and quantify drift with reference-free metrics capturing spatial localization and structural consistency of attribution maps.
